definition{
  name: rootAt,
  parameters: [cursor : Cursor, slot : RootSlot],
  result: Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle,
  body: (match slot
    | RootSlot.programBlocks => (project (project (project cursor program) blocks) root)
    | RootSlot.parameterTypes => (project (project cursor parameterTypes) root)
    | RootSlot.localTypes => (project (project cursor localTypes) root)
    | RootSlot.instructions => (project (project cursor instructions) root)
    | RootSlot.lastUses => (project (project cursor lastUses) root)
    | RootSlot.lifetimes => (project (project cursor lifetimes) root)
    | RootSlot.capturesA => (project (project cursor capturesA) root)
    | RootSlot.capturesB => (project (project cursor capturesB) root)
    | RootSlot.bucketSlots => (project (project cursor bucketSlots) root)
    | RootSlot.initialRelease => (project cursor initialRelease)
    | RootSlot.blockRecord => (project cursor blockRecord)
    | RootSlot.instructionRecord => (project cursor instructionRecord)
    | RootSlot.bucketRecord => (project cursor bucketRecord)
    | RootSlot.literal => (project cursor literal)
    | RootSlot.auxFirst => (project cursor auxFirst)
    | RootSlot.auxSecond => (project cursor auxSecond)
    | RootSlot.control => (project cursor control)),
  axioms: [],
  executable: true
}

definition{
  name: taskWork,
  parameters: [task : Task],
  result: UInt64,
  body: (match task
    | Task.stage stageValue => (match stageValue
      | Stage.initialize => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.admissionInitialization))
      | Stage.awaiting => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.idle))
      | Stage.dispatch => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.control))
      | Stage.ack => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.acknowledgment))
      | Stage.parameterType => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.indexedStart))
      | Stage.snapshotParameters => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.heapRetain))
      | Stage.scalar => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.scalarCheck))
      | Stage.capture => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.captureCheck))
      | Stage.captureUse => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.indexedStart))
      | Stage.literalStart => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.builderControl))
      | Stage.literalSpan => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.heapAllocate))
      | Stage.literalRead => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.builderControl))
      | Stage.literalFinish => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.builderControl))
      | Stage.literalJoin => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.sequenceBegin))
      | Stage.literalJoinDone => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.literalDropLeaf => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.instructionField index => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.instructionAttach => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.indexedStart))
      | Stage.instructionType => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.indexedStart))
      | Stage.instructionCleanup index => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.blockReturn => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.sealCheck))
      | Stage.lifetime => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.lifetimePlanning))
      | Stage.bucketAppend => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.indexedStart))
      | Stage.bucketField index => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.bucketAttach => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.indexedStart))
      | Stage.bucketCleanup => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.bucketCleanupSlots => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.blockField index => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.blockAttach => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.indexedStart))
      | Stage.blockCount => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.control))
      | Stage.blockCleanup index => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.sealCheck => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.sealCheck))
      | Stage.sealFinish => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.smallControl))
      | Stage.ready => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.idle)))
    | Task.table progress slot after => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.tableMutation))
    | Task.record recordCursor slot after => (Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::fee (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeWork::Operation.metadataMutation))

  executable: true
